        <span class="ct-node-content">
          <span class="ct-node-label">Think Before Sharing</span>
          <span class="ct-node-title-full">Can You Trust What Found You Online?</span>
          <span class="ct-node-summary">Critical thinking in the age of social media and AI is no longer just the ability to spot a false headline. It is the habit of asking how a claim reached you, who benefits from your reaction, what evidence would change your mind, and whether a machine-generated answer is being treated as a shortcut for judgement.</span>
        </span>
      </a>
